# Skills · L · part 29

836 entries, level-design-lead to lh-pairing. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **level-design-lead**: Claude's standing role as lead level designer of Breach -- everything that makes a level: generation (the graph grammar, departments, cycle types, embedding, gates), hand-authored levels, the level format and its writer, pieces, vents and cells as level data, caverns and other archetypes, and the LLM-authored-rules experiment. Load at the start of ANY session that touches levelgen, a level file, level_lib / level_loader, gen_ship, the map editor's level output or milestones 11 and 12, before reading the code. ([eriiiko/breach](https://github.com/eriiiko/breach/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/level-design-lead) · ★2)
